**1.3. Danh phap cua thu6c**

_**Danh phap hoa hrc:**_ m6i thu6c c6 danh phap h6a h9c rieng dva tren cong thuc va
cfru t?o h6a h9c. Danh phap h6a h9c mo ta chinh xac cong thuc phan tu cua thu6c.

Vi dl,l: danh phap h6a h9c cua "paracetamol" la _"N-(_ 4-hydroxyphenyl)acetamide".
_**Danh phap chung quiJc**_ _**ti INN (International Nonproprietary Name):**_ la danh
phap g9i ten ho?t chfrt mang tinh th6ng nhfrt tren th€ gi&i. M6i ho?t chfrt a€u c6 ten
chung qu6c t€ rieng bi�t va OUQ'C tfrt ca cac nu&c g9i chung. Ten g9i nay cilng se la ten
"generic" cua thu6c.
